We identify a systematic failure mode in this setting: reformulating harmful requests as forced-choice MCQs, where all options are unsafe, can systematically bypass refusal behavior, even in models that consistently reject equivalent open-ended prompts. Across 14 proprietary and open-source models, we show that forced-choice constraints sharply increase policy-violating responses. Notably, for human-authored MCQs, violation rates follow an inverted U-shaped trend with respect to structural constraint strength, peaking under intermediate task specifications, whereas MCQs generated by high-capability models yield near-saturation violation rates across constraints and exhibit strong cross-model transferability. Our findings reveal that current safety evaluations substantially underestimate risks in structured task settings and highlight constrained decision-making as a critical and underexplored surface for alignment failures.

</details>


### [75] [x1: Learning to Think Adaptively Across Languages and Cultures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16917)
*Yangfan Ye,Xiaocheng Feng,Xiachong Feng,Yichong Huang,Zekun Yuan,Lei Huang,Weitao Ma,Qichen Hong,Yunfei Lu,Dandan Tu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: x1模型系列通过自适应选择最有利的语言进行逐实例推理，突破了传统大语言模型仅用单一主导语言推理的限制，在多语言数学推理和文化相关任务中表现出色


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数大语言模型(LLMs)忽略了语言多样性的重要价值，仅用单一主导语言进行推理，而不同语言编码了不同的抽象概念和归纳先验，这种多样性对推理能力有重要影响

Method: 构建x1模型系列，在不扩展模型知识边界的前提下，通过对比同一输入的不同语言推理轨迹来训练模型，使其能够自适应地为每个实例选择最有利的推理语言

Result: 实验表明自适应多语言推理在多语言数学推理和文化相关任务中都有显著优势。缩放定律并不总是有效：虽然缩放减少了数学推理等程序性领域的跨语言差异，但文化相关语言在文化基础任务中的优势仍然存在，使用相关语言能够更高效准确地回忆文化知识

Conclusion: 语言选择是推理的功能性组成部分，这一发现对构建更通用、更具全球竞争力的推理模型具有重要意义，挑战了简单的缩放定律观点，强调了语言多样性在AI推理中的关键作用

Abstract: Languages encode distinct abstractions and inductive priors, yet most large language models (LLMs) overlook this diversity by reasoning in a single dominant language. In this work, we introduce x1, a family of reasoning models that can adaptively reason in an advantageous language on a per-instance basis. To isolate the effect of reasoning-language choice, x1 is constructed without expanding the model's knowledge boundaries and is trained by contrasting linguistically distinct reasoning trajectories for the same input. Our extensive experiments demonstrate the benefits of adaptive multilingual reasoning across multilingual mathematical reasoning and culturally grounded tasks. Moreover, our results challenge a simplistic view of scaling laws: while scaling reduces cross-lingual disparities in procedural domains such as math reasoning, it does not eliminate the advantages of culture-associated languages in culturally grounded tasks, as we empirically show that such reasoning enables more efficient and accurate cultural knowledge recall. Overall, our findings establish language choice as a functional component of reasoning, with implications for building more generalist and globally competent reasoning models.

</details>


### [76] [Freshness-Aware Prioritized Experience Replay for LLM/VLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16918)
*Weiyu Ma,Yongcheng Zeng,Yan Song,Xinyu Cui,Jian Zhao,Xuhui Liu,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Freshness-Aware PER方法通过引入基于年龄的指数衰减机制，成功地将优先级经验回放应用于大语言模型和视觉语言模型的强化学习中，显著提高了样本效率和多步任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于策略的强化学习算法（如PPO、GRPO、REINFORCE++）在训练大型语言模型时存在样本效率低下的问题，因为它们对收集的轨迹只进行一次梯度更新后就丢弃。特别是在需要多轮环境交互的智能体任务中，这种浪费尤为严重。虽然经典强化学习中的经验回放能够提高样本效率，但直接将其应用于LLMs时会因策略快速演变导致存储的优先级信息过时，使旧的高优先级轨迹长期主导采样而变得无效。

Method: 提出Freshness-Aware PER方法，通过在任何基于PER的优先级上添加一个基于有效样本大小分析的乘法指数年龄衰减因子，解决优先级过时问题。这种方法使较新的经验获得更高采样权重，从而适应LLMs策略的快速演变特性。

Result: 在8个多步智能体、推理和数学竞赛任务上（使用0.5B、3B和7B模型）进行评估。Freshness-Aware PER显著优于基于策略的基线方法：在NQ Search上提升46%，Sokoban上提升367%，VLM FrozenLake上提升133%。而不带年龄衰减的标准PER方法会持续降低性能。

Conclusion: Freshness-Aware PER是第一个成功将优先级经验回放应用于LLM/VLM强化学习的工作。它通过解决优先级过时问题，有效提高了样本效率，在多种复杂的多步任务上实现了显著的性能提升，为大型语言模型的强化学习训练提供了一种更高效的替代方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has achieved impressive success in post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), with on-policy algorithms such as PPO, GRPO, and REINFORCE++ serving as the dominant paradigm. However, these methods discard all collected trajectories after a single gradient update, resulting in poor sample efficiency, particularly wasteful for agentic tasks where multi-turn environment interactions are expensive. While Experience Replay drives sample efficiency in classic RL by allowing agents to reuse past trajectories and prioritize informative ones, directly applying Prioritized Experience Replay (PER) to LLMs fails. The rapid policy evolution of billion-parameter models renders stored priorities stale, causing old high-priority trajectories to dominate sampling long after they have become uninformative. We propose Freshness-Aware PER, which addresses this priority staleness problem by augmenting any PER-based priority with a multiplicative exponential age decay grounded in effective sample size analysis. To the best of our knowledge, Freshness-Aware PER is the first work to successfully apply PER to LLM/VLM reinforcement learning. We evaluate on eight multi-step agentic, reasoning, and math competition tasks with 0.5B, 3B, and 7B models. Freshness-Aware PER significantly outperforms on-policy baselines, achieving +46% on NQ Search, +367% on Sokoban, and +133% on VLM FrozenLake, while standard PER without age decay consistently degrades performance. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Vision-CAIR/Freshness-Aware-PER.

</details>


### [77] [MeasHalu: Mitigation of Scientific Measurement Hallucinations for Large Language Models with Enhanced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16929)
*Ruijun Huang,Zhiqiao Kang,Yuxuan Zhu,Junxiong Li,Jiahao Zhao,Minghuan Tan,Feng Jiang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeasHalu是一个通过增强推理和针对性优化来减轻科学测量幻觉的新框架，显著降低LLMs在科学文献测量提取中的幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从科学文献中准确提取测量数据对AI4Science至关重要，但大语言模型经常出现严重幻觉，损害了自动化科学文档理解系统的可靠性。

Method: 提出了测量幻觉的细粒度分类法，涵盖数量、单位、修饰词和关系等错误类型。采用两阶段推理感知的微调策略，结合增强的科学数据和基于过程的监督。引入渐进式奖励课程来惩罚特定幻觉类型。

Result: MeasHalu在MeasEval基准测试中显著降低了幻觉率并提高了整体准确性。

Conclusion: 这项工作为自动化科学知识提取中的关键瓶颈提供了针对性解决方案，促进了更可信和可扩展的机器辅助科学文献分析。

Abstract: The accurate extraction of scientific measurements from literature is a critical yet challenging task in AI4Science, enabling large-scale analysis and integration of quantitative research findings. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently exhibit severe hallucinations, which significantly undermine the reliability of automated scientific document understanding systems. To address this problem, we propose MeasHalu, a novel framework for mitigating scientific measurement hallucinations through enhanced reasoning and targeted optimization. We first present a fine-grained taxonomy of measurement-specific hallucinations, categorizing errors across quantities, units, modifiers, and relations. Our approach incorporates a two-stage reasoning-aware fine-tuning strategy using augmented scientific data and process-based supervision. Furthermore, we introduce a progressive reward curriculum designed to penalize specific hallucination types, significantly improving extraction faithfulness. Experimental results demonstrate that MeasHalu substantially reduces hallucination rates and improves overall accuracy on the MeasEval benchmark. This work provides a targeted solution to a key bottleneck in automated scientific knowledge extraction, facilitating more trustworthy and scalable machine-assisted scientific literature analysis.

</details>


### [78] [No One Fits All: From Fixed Prompting to Learned Routing in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16937)
*Wei-Chi Wu,Sheng-Lun Wei,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估了多语言LLM中翻译提示策略在不同语言和任务中的效果，发现无单一最优策略，并提出了基于学习的提示策略选择方法，使用轻量级分类器预测每个实例的最佳提示方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 翻译提示在多语言LLMs中广泛应用，但其效果因语言和任务而异，需要系统评估不同提示策略的有效性，并开发能自适应选择最佳策略的方法。

Method: 在十个不同资源水平的语言和四个基准测试中评估提示策略，将提示策略选择建模为学习决策问题，引入轻量级分类器来预测每个实例是使用原生提示还是翻译提示更优。

Result: 翻译对低资源语言有明显益处（即使翻译质量不完美），高资源语言获益很少；基于提示的自路由方法表现不如显式翻译；提出的分类器在四个基准测试中相比固定策略取得统计显著改进，并能泛化到训练中未见过的任务格式。

Conclusion: 语言资源水平（而非仅翻译质量）决定何时翻译有益；通过学习的提示策略选择可实现跨语言和任务的自适应性能提升，轻量级分类器能有效预测最佳提示策略。

Abstract: Translation-based prompting is widely used in multilingual LLMs, yet its effectiveness varies across languages and tasks. We evaluate prompting strategies across ten languages of different resource levels and four benchmarks. Our analysis shows that no single strategy is universally optimal. Translation strongly benefits low-resource languages even when translation quality is imperfect, high-resource languages gain little, and prompt-based self-routing underperforms explicit translation. Motivated by these findings, we formulate prompting strategy selection as a learned decision problem and introduce lightweight classifiers that predict whether native or translation-based prompting is optimal for each instance. The classifiers achieve statistically significant improvements over fixed strategies across four benchmarks and generalize to unseen task formats not observed during training. Further analysis reveals that language resource level, rather than translation quality alone, determines when translation is beneficial.

</details>


### [79] [MNAFT: modality neuron-aware fine-tuning of multimodal large language models for image translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16943)
*Bo Li,Ningyuan Deng,Tianyu Dong,Shaobo Wang,Shaolin Zhu,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出模态神经元感知微调(MNAFT)方法，通过识别和选择性地微调语言特定神经元和语言无关神经元来提升多模态大语言模型在图像翻译任务中的性能，避免知识遗忘并提高泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在图像翻译任务中难以有效捕捉图像中的细粒度文本信息，导致视觉文本输入与文本输入/输出之间存在模态鸿沟。现有基于指令微调的方法存在参数冗余风险，可能损害预训练知识的保持并影响泛化性能。

Method: 提出模态神经元感知微调(MNAFT)方法：1)通过指令驱动的激活分析识别视觉和语言模块中的语言无关神经元和语言特定神经元；2)评估这些神经元在不同翻译任务中的重要性；3)对与目标任务相关的选定层中，仅更新语言特定和语言无关神经元的参数，保留其他神经元和层的编码知识。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，MNAFT显著优于最先进的图像翻译方法，包括级联模型、标准全微调和参数高效微调技术。还提供了神经激活可视化和聚类模式的全面分析，揭示不同神经元群体在跨模态理解和促进准确语言特定翻译中的作用。

Conclusion: MNAFT通过利用多模态大语言模型中神经元的专门角色，提供了一种有效的图像翻译微调方法，既保留了预训练知识，又提高了特定任务的性能，为跨模态理解提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown impressive capabilities, yet they often struggle to effectively capture the fine-grained textual information within images crucial for accurate image translation. This often leads to a modality gap between visual text inputs and textual inputs/outputs for image translation. Existing methods, primarily relying on instruction fine-tuning, risk parameter redundancy of pre-trained knowledge, hindering generalization performance. To address this, we introduce modality neuron-aware fine-tuning (MNAFT), a novel approach that takes advantage of the specialized roles of individual neurons within MLLMs for enhanced image translation. MNAFT identifies language-agnostic and language-specific neurons in both vision and language modules through an instruction-driven activation analysis, evaluating their importance in various translation tasks. We then perform selective fine-tuning, updating only the parameters of language-specific and language-agnostic neurons within the selected layers relevant to the target task, while preserving the knowledge encoded in other neurons and layers. Our extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that MNAFT significantly outperforms state-of-the-art image translation methods, including cascaded models, standard full fine-tuning, and parameter-efficient tuning techniques. Furthermore, we provide comprehensive analysis, including visualizations of neuron activations and clustering patterns, to offer insights into the roles of different neuron groups in mediating cross-modal understanding and facilitating accurate language-specific translation.

</details>


### [80] [Bolzano: Case Studies in LLM-Assisted Mathematical Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16989)
*Jan Grebík,Pavel Hubáček,Martin Koutecký,Matěj Kripner,Václav Rozhoň,Robert Šámal,Adrián Zámečník*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Bolzano多智能体LLM系统在数学和理论计算机科学的六个问题上取得了新成果，其中四个达到可发表水平，三个基本由系统自主完成，证明LLM能够有意义地贡献于数学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型（LLMs）在数学研究中的潜力，补充Bubeck等人和Woodruff等人的近期报告，验证LLMs能否对数学研究做出有意义的贡献。

Method: 使用Bolzano开源多智能体LLM系统，该系统协调并行证明者智能体与验证者智能体之间的多轮交互，同时维护跨轮次持久的知识库。采用Feng等人的意义-自主性分类法对结果进行评估。

Result: 在数学和理论计算机科学的六个问题上报告了新结果：四个结果达到可发表研究水平，其中三个结果基本由Bolzano系统自主产生。这提供了LLMs能够对数学研究做出有意义贡献的证据。

Conclusion: LLMs能够有意义地贡献于数学研究，Bolzano多智能体系统在多个数学和理论计算机科学问题上展现了研究能力，其中部分成果达到了可发表水平且具有相当程度的自主性。

Abstract: We report new results on six problems in mathematics and theoretical computer science, produced with the assistance of Bolzano, an open-source multi-agent LLM system. Bolzano orchestrates rounds of interaction between parallel prover agents and a verifier agent while maintaining a persistent knowledge base that is carried across rounds. Classified using the significance-autonomy taxonomy of Feng et al., four of the six results reach the level of publishable research, and three of the six were produced essentially autonomously by Bolzano. Our results provide evidence that LLMs can contribute meaningfully to mathematical research, complementing recent reports by Bubeck et al., Woodruff et al., and others.

</details>


### [81] [SPS: Steering Probability Squeezing for Better Exploration in Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16995)
*Yifu Huo,Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Shunjie Xing,Peinan Feng,Tongran Liu,Qiaozhi He,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Jingbo Zhu,Zhengtao Yu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Steering Probability Squeezing (SPS)的新训练范式，通过在强化学习和逆向强化学习之间交替使用，解决强化学习中概率质量过度集中在狭窄子集的问题，以增强推理模型的探索能力并提升多样本性能(Pass@k)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习(RL)训练范式在训练推理导向模型时，虽然能有效提高单样本成功率(Pass@1)，但限制了多样推理轨迹的探索，这对多样本性能(Pass@k)至关重要。作者发现这一限制源于根本性的"挤压效应"，即概率质量过度集中在少量高奖励轨迹的狭窄子集上，限制了真正的探索能力。

Method: 提出了Steering Probability Squeezing (SPS)训练范式，它将传统的强化学习与逆向强化学习(IRL)交织使用。SPS将在线策略轨迹作为演示，使用IRL显式地重塑诱导的轨迹分布，从而在不引入外部监督的情况下增强探索能力。

Result: 在五个常用推理基准测试上的实验表明，SPS能够实现更好的探索并提高Pass@k性能。此外，作者还提供了对RL学习动态的分析，并确定了Pass@k的经验上界，揭示了RL基推理模型中固有的探索限制。

Conclusion: 强化学习和逆向强化学习的交替使用为扩展推理导向大语言模型的探索能力提供了一条有效途径。SPS通过重塑轨迹分布成功缓解了概率质量过度集中的问题，提升了多样本推理性能，同时为理解RL基推理模型的探索极限提供了理论分析框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training reasoning-oriented models by leveraging rule-based reward signals. However, RL training typically tends to improve single-sample success rates (i.e., Pass@1) while offering limited exploration of diverse reasoning trajectories, which is crucial for multi-sample performance (i.e., Pass@k). Our preliminary analysis reveals that this limitation stems from a fundamental squeezing effect, whereby probability mass is excessively concentrated on a narrow subset of high-reward trajectories, restricting genuine exploration and constraining attainable performance under RL training. To address this issue, in this work, we propose Steering Probability Squeezing (SPS), a training paradigm that interleaves conventional RL with inverse reinforcement learning (IRL). SPS treats on-policy rollouts as demonstrations and employs IRL to explicitly reshape the induced trajectory distribution, thereby enhancing exploration without introducing external supervision. Experiments on five commonly used reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SPS can enable better exploration and improve Pass@k. Beyond algorithmic contributions, we provide an analysis of RL learning dynamics and identify an empirical upper bound on Pass@k, shedding light on intrinsic exploration limits in RL-based reasoning models. Our findings suggest that alternating between RL and IRL offers an effective pathway toward extending the exploration capacity of reasoning-oriented large language models.

</details>


### [82] [BIASEDTALES-ML: A Multilingual Dataset for Analyzing Narrative Attribute Distributions in LLM-Generated Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17008)
*Yuxuan Ouyang,yingfeng luo,JingBo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BiasedTales-ML数据集，一个包含约35万篇儿童故事的平行语料库，涵盖8种不同语言。研究通过结构化生成-提取流水线分析多语言叙事生成模式，发现英语中心评估在跨语言环境中存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型(LLMs)越来越多用于生成叙事内容(如儿童故事)，这些内容在社会和文化学习中扮演重要角色。尽管AI安全和对齐受到关注，但现有评估主要关注英语，跨语言对齐行为的泛化研究不足，特别是在多元文化背景下。

Method: 1) 构建BiasedTales-ML数据集：约35万篇儿童故事，覆盖8种类型学和文化多样的语言，采用全排列提示设计；2) 提出结构化生成-提取流水线；3) 开发多维分布分析框架，考察叙事属性在不同语言、模型和社会条件下的变化。

Result: 研究发现：1) 叙事生成模式存在显著的跨语言变异性，英语中观察到的分布特征在其他语言(特别是低资源语言)中并不总是相似；2) 在叙事层面，识别出涉及角色、场景和主题强调的重复结构模式，这些在不同语言环境中表现出差异；3) 英语中心评估在描述多语言环境中基于社会的叙事生成方面存在局限性。

Conclusion: 研究强调了英语中心评估在跨语言叙事分析中的不足，为多语言AI对齐和社会文化学习研究提供了新的数据集、方法和分析框架。作者发布了数据集、代码和交互式可视化工具，支持未来多语言叙事分析和评估研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate narrative content, including children's stories, which play an important role in social and cultural learning. Despite growing interest in AI safety and alignment, most existing evaluations focus primarily on English, leaving the cross-lingual generalization of aligned behavior underexplored. In this work, we introduce BiasedTales-ML, a large-scale parallel corpus of approximately 350,000 children's stories generated across eight typologically and culturally diverse languages using a full-permutation prompting design. We propose a structured generator-extractor pipeline and a multi-dimensional distributional analysis framework to examine how narrative attributes vary across languages, models, and social conditions. Our analysis reveals substantial cross-lingual variability in narrative generation patterns, indicating that distributions observed in English do not always exhibit similar characteristics in other languages, particularly in lower-resource settings. At the narrative level, we identify recurring structural patterns involving character roles, settings, and thematic emphasis, which manifest differently across linguistic contexts. These findings highlight the limitations of English-centric evaluation for characterizing socially grounded narrative generation in multilingual settings. We release the dataset, code, and an interactive visualization tool to support future research on multilingual narrative analysis and evaluation.

</details>


### [83] [Improving LLM Code Reasoning via Semantic Equivalence Self-Play with Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17010)
*Antonio Valerio Miceli Barone,Poon Tsz Nok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个用于Haskell程序语义等价性的自博弈框架，通过形式验证指导生成器和评估器之间的对抗训练，并发布了一个包含约28k验证Haskell程序的数据集OpInstruct-HSx。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发能够准确判断Haskell程序语义等价性的方法，传统方法可能难以处理复杂的语义推理，因此需要结合形式验证和机器学习的方法来提升程序等价性判断的准确性。

Method: 采用自博弈框架，包含生成器和评估器进行对抗训练。使用Liquid Haskell形式验证来验证程序等价性，执行生成的程序作为反例来证明非等价性，并通过难度感知课程学习组织训练过程。

Result: 实验表明评估器在下游任务中迁移效果良好，在EquiBench上获得最高13.3个百分点的准确率提升，在PySecDB上也有稳定提升。消融研究显示，虽然非等价监督提供了数据量，但等价证明对模型的推理能力有独特贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架成功结合了形式验证和对抗训练来提升Haskell程序语义等价性判断，发布的OpInstruct-HSx数据集和训练流程为相关研究提供了有价值的资源，展示了形式证明在机器学习模型推理能力培养中的关键作用。

Abstract: We introduce a self-play framework for semantic equivalence in Haskell, utilizing formal verification to guide adversarial training between a generator and an evaluator. The framework leverages Liquid Haskell proofs for validating equivalence and execution-based counterexamples for inequivalence, organized via a difficulty-aware curriculum. To facilitate this, we release \textbf{OpInstruct-HSx}, a synthetic dataset of $\approx$28k validated Haskell programs. Empirical experiments show that our evaluator transfers effectively to downstream tasks, achieving up to 13.3pp accuracy gain on EquiBench and consistent gains on PySecDB. Ablation studies on the SEQ-SINQ regimes indicate that while inequivalence supervision provides data volume, equivalence proofs are uniquely responsible for the model's reasoning capabilities. The entire training pipeline and dataset are publicly released on GitHub and Hugging Face respectively.

</details>


### [84] [Beyond Black-Box Labels: Interpretable Criteria for Diagnosing SubjectiveNLP Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17022)
*Nisrine Rair,Alban Goupil,Valeriu Vrabie,Emmanuel Chochoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种模式级别的诊断方法，用于在确定金标签之前审计专家设计的标注模式，通过分析多标注者的标准判断来区分两种失败模式：标准不稳定和类别边界模糊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观NLP数据集通常将标注者判断聚合为单一金标签，这使得难以诊断分歧是由于标准不清晰、区别被忽略，还是合理的多元性。现有方法无法有效区分这些不同的分歧来源。

Method: 提出模式级别的诊断方法，使用多标注者的标准判断（而非最终标签）来审计标注模式。该方法区分两种失败模式：1) 标准不稳定：难以操作化的边界；2) 系统性重叠：互斥类别之间的边界模糊。应用于商业文档的说服价值提取任务。

Result: 应用该方法发现分歧不是分散的：不稳定性集中在少数标准上，而近一半的覆盖句子激活了多个类别。这些信号与领域专家的分歧点相一致，验证了诊断方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种基于证据的审计工具，可用于收紧标注指南、修订类别结构或重新考虑标注范式，有助于在标注过程中早期发现问题并提高数据质量。

Abstract: Subjective NLP datasets typically aggregate annotator judgments into a single gold label, making it difficult to diagnose whether disagreement reflects unclear criteria, collapsed distinctions, or legitimate plurality. We propose a \emph{schema-level diagnostic} for auditing expert-designed annotation schemas \emph{prior to} gold-label commitment, using only multi-annotator criterion judgments. The diagnostic separates two failure modes: unstable criteria with hard-to-operationalize boundaries, and systematic overlap that blurs the boundaries between mutually exclusive categories. Applied to persuasive value extraction in commercial documents, we find that disagreement is not diffuse: instability concentrates in a few criteria, while nearly half of covered sentences activate multiple categories. These signals align with where domain experts disagree, yielding an evidence-based audit for tightening guidelines, revising category structure, or reconsidering the annotation paradigm.

</details>


### [85] [Where is the Mind? Persona Vectors and LLM Individuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17031)
*Pierre Beckmann,Patrick Butlin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型的个体化问题探讨了哪些与之相关的实体应被识别为心智。作者通过机制可解释性方法，结合最近关于人格向量、人格空间和突发失调的实证研究，提出了三种最强候选观点：虚拟实例观点、两种新观点（虚拟实例-人格观点和模型-人格观点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机是解决大型语言模型中的个体化问题，即确定哪些与LLM相关的实体应被视为心智。这个问题在人工智能哲学和认知科学中具有重要意义，特别是在LLM表现出类似人格行为的情况下。

Method: 作者采用机制可解释性方法，特别关注最近关于人格向量、人格空间和突发失调的实证研究。他们系统地分析了人格文献，围绕LLM中人格内部结构的三个假设进行组织，并评估不同观点。

Result: 作者论证了虚拟实例观点的合理性，认为注意力流在令牌时间上维持了准心理连接。同时，他们提出了两种基于人格的新观点：虚拟实例-人格观点和模型-人格观点，并展示了这些基于人格的观点是有前景的替代方案。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型的个体化问题有多个有前景的解决方案。虚拟实例观点基于注意力机制的支持，而两种新的人格观点（虚拟实例-人格和模型-人格）同样具有说服力。这表明LLM的心智识别可能需要多元化的理论框架。

Abstract: The individuation problem for large language models asks which entities associated with them, if any, should be identified as minds. We approach this problem through mechanistic interpretability, engaging in particular with recent empirical work on persona vectors, persona space, and emergent misalignment. We argue that three views are the strongest candidates: the virtual instance view and two new views we introduce, the (virtual) instance-persona view and the model-persona view. First, we argue for the virtual instance view on the grounds that attention streams sustain quasi-psychological connections across token-time. Then we present the persona literature, organised around three hypotheses about the internal structure underlying personas in LLMs, and show that the two persona-based views are promising alternatives.

</details>


### [86] [Dynamic Emotion and Personality Profiling for Multimodal Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17037)
*Li Zheng,Yanyi Luo,Hao Fei,Yuzhe Ding,Yujie Huang,Fei Li,Chong Teng,Donghong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Rel-DDEP框架，通过多模型多提示标注方案创建了欺骗、情感和人格多模态联合检测数据集DDEP，并采用可靠性加权融合实现了三项任务的联合检测，显著提升了各项任务的性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗检测对信息安全和舆论分析具有重要意义，但现有方法缺乏样本级别的动态情感和人格标注，限制了检测性能的提升。

Method: 1) 提出创新的多模型多提示标注方案和严格的标签质量评估标准，建立了DDEP多模态联合检测数据集；2) 提出Rel-DDEP自适应可靠性加权融合框架，通过将模态特征映射到高维高斯分布空间量化不确定性，进行可靠性加权融合，并包含对齐模块和排序约束模块。

Result: 在MDPE和DDEP数据集上的实验表明，Rel-DDEP显著优于现有最先进的基线模型：欺骗检测F1分数提升2.53%，情感检测提升2.66%，人格检测提升9.30%。

Conclusion: 实验充分验证了为每个样本标注动态情感和人格标签的必要性，以及可靠性加权融合方法的有效性，为解决缺乏样本级动态标注的问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Deception detection is of great significance for ensuring information security and conducting public opinion analysis, with personality factors and emotion cues playing a critical role. However, existing methods lack sample-level dynamic annotations for emotions and personality.In this paper, we propose an innovative multi-model multi-prompt annotation scheme and a strict label quality evaluation standard, and establish a multimodal joint detection dataset DDEP for deception, emotion, and personality. Meanwhile, we propose Rel-DDEP, an adaptive reliability-weighted fusion framework. Our framework quantifies uncertainty by mapping modal features to a high-dimensional Gaussian distribution space. It then performs reliability-weighted fusion and incorporates an alignment module and a sorting constraint module to achieve joint detection of deception, emotion, and personality. Experimental results on the MDPE and DDEP datasets show that our Rel-DDEP significantly outperforms the existing state-of-the-art baseline models in three tasks. The F1 score of the deception detection increases by 2.53%, that of the emotion detection increases by 2.66%, and that of the personality detection increases by 9.30%. The experiments fully verify the necessity of annotating dynamic emotion and personality labels for each sample and the effectiveness of reliability-weighted fusion.

</details>


### [87] [Efficient Task Adaptation in Large Language Models via Selective Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17051)
*Weijie Wan,Jiangjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于参数重要性评估的微调方法，通过区分参数对通用语言能力和特定领域任务的重要性，将参数分为"核心参数"和"非核心参数"，在微调时只更新非核心参数以减轻灾难性遗忘


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在针对特定领域任务进行微调时，预训练阶段积累的通用知识往往因参数更新而被部分覆盖或遗忘，严重限制了模型的泛化能力和迁移性。传统微调策略在整个参数空间上训练，忽略了模型参数的异质性，即有些参数对通用任务极其重要，而有些参数对特定任务更敏感。

Method: 创新性地提出参数元素重要性评估方法，通过区分参数对通用语言能力任务和特定领域任务的重要性，将参数分为"核心参数"和"非核心参数"。在微调过程中固定核心参数，只微调非核心参数。

Result: 在科学、医学和物理任务上使用GPT-J和LLaMA-3进行广泛实验，结果表明该方法既能缓解灾难性遗忘问题，又能增强模型的适应性。

Conclusion: 通过参数重要性评估区分核心与非核心参数并选择性微调的方法，有效地平衡了模型在特定任务上的适应性与通用知识的保留，为LLM的微调提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated excellent performance in general language understanding, generation and other tasks. However, when fine-tuning for specific domain tasks, the general knowledge accumulated in the pre-training phase is often partially overwritten or forgotten due to parameter updates, which severely limits the generalization ability and transferability of LLMs. Traditional fine-tuning strategies mostly train on the entire parameter space, ignoring the heterogeneity of model parameters, that is, some parameters are extremely important for general tasks, while other parameters are more sensitive to specific tasks. To alleviate the above problems, this paper innovatively proposes a parameter element importance evaluation method, which divides parameters into "core parameters" and "non-core parameters" by distinguishing the importance of parameters for general language ability tasks and specific domain tasks, and fixes the core parameters during fine-tuning, and only fine-tunes the non-core parameters. Extensive experiments on scientific, medical and physical tasks using GPT-J and LLaMA-3 show that our method can mitigate catastrophic forgetting while enhancing the adaptability of the model.

</details>


### [88] [Stability-Weighted Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17068)
*Yue Wu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稳定性加权的解码策略（SWD），用于扩散大语言模型，通过考虑token的时域稳定性来改进并行文本生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型的解码策略依赖单个去噪步骤计算的静态置信度指标，忽略了时间历史信息，容易导致不稳定token的过早去掩码，从而影响生成质量。

Method: 理论证明了token的时域不稳定性（通过连续预测分布的KL散度量化的）为其与剩余掩码上下文互信息的严格下界，基于此提出了稳定性加权的解码策略（SWD）。SWD将时域稳定性纳入token评分，作为任意基于评分解码策略的通用调节器。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理基准测试中，SWD在代表性评分指标和选择策略上一致提高了生成准确性，并在不同加速比下表现出卓越的鲁棒性，显著优于标准基线方法。

Conclusion: 时域不稳定性是衡量token去掩码安全性的有效指标，提出的SWD策略无需训练、即插即用，能显著改善扩散大语言模型的并行文本生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) enable parallel text generation by iteratively denoising a fully masked sequence, unmasking a subset of masked tokens at each step. Existing decoding strategies rely on static confidence metrics computed at a single denoising step, ignoring temporal history and often leading to premature unmasking of unstable tokens. In this work, we theoretically establish that a token's temporal instability, quantified by the KL divergence between consecutive prediction distributions, provides a strict lower bound on its mutual information with the remaining masked context, indicating that temporally unstable tokens are inherently unsafe to unmask. Based on this insight, we propose Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD), a training-free, plug-and-play strategy that incorporates temporal stability into token scoring and acts as a universal modulator for arbitrary score-based decoding policies. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SWD consistently improves generation accuracy across representative scoring metrics and selection policies, and exhibits exceptional robustness, maintaining a significant performance lead over standard baselines across varying acceleration ratios.

</details>


### [89] [Abstain-R1: Calibrated Abstention and Post-Refusal Clarification via Verifiable RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17073)
*Skylar Zhai,Jingcheng Liang,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RLVR奖励方法训练Abstain-R1模型，在不可回答查询上实现校准的弃权与澄清，同时保持可回答查询的良好性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化微调方法虽提升LLM推理能力，但会导致模型猜测或产生幻觉来回答不可回答的查询。现有弃权方法要么训练模型生成通用拒绝回复，要么鼓励后续澄清但不验证这些澄清是否识别出关键缺失信息

Method: 提出澄清感知的RLVR奖励，在可回答查询上奖励正确答案，同时在不可回答查询上联合优化显式弃权和语义对齐的拒绝后澄清

Result: 训练的3B参数模型Abstain-R1在Abstain-Test、Abstain-QA和SelfAware等基准测试中显著优于基础模型，在不可回答查询行为上与DeepSeek-R1等更大系统竞争，表明校准的弃权和澄清可通过可验证奖励习得而非仅依赖规模

Conclusion: 可靠模型不仅应在不可回答查询上弃权，还应解释缺失什么信息。通过RLVR奖励方法训练的小规模模型可学习校准的弃权和澄清能力，表明该能力可通过结构化训练而非仅通过扩大模型规模实现

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning improves the reasoning ability of large language models, but it can also encourage them to answer unanswerable queries by guessing or hallucinating missing information. Existing abstention methods either train models to produce generic refusals or encourage follow-up clarifications without verifying whether those clarifications identify the key missing information. We study queries that are clear in meaning but cannot be reliably resolved from the given information, and argue that a reliable model should not only abstain, but also explain what is missing. We propose a clarification-aware RLVR reward that, while rewarding correct answers on answerable queries, jointly optimizes explicit abstention and semantically aligned post-refusal clarification on unanswerable queries. Using this reward, we train Abstain-R1, a 3B model that improves abstention and clarification on unanswerable queries while preserving strong performance on answerable ones. Experiments on Abstain-Test, Abstain-QA, and SelfAware show that Abstain-R1 substantially improves over its base model and achieves unanswerable-query behavior competitive with larger systems including DeepSeek-R1, suggesting that calibrated abstention and clarification can be learned through verifiable rewards rather than emerging from scale alone.

</details>


### [90] [Auditing Support Strategies in LLMs through Grounded Multi-Turn Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17079)
*Michelle Star,Andrew Aquilina,Yu-Ru Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个多轮模拟框架来评估支持性大语言模型（LLMs），通过将Reddit社群的求助叙述分解为有序片段逐轮呈现给模型，并分析模型支持策略随用户苦恼估计值变化的系统性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大多数对支持性LLMs的评估依赖于单轮、完全指定的提示，但用户在实际寻求社交支持时是逐步披露情况的，这种评估方式与实际使用场景存在脱节。因此需要开发能模拟真实多轮交互的评估框架。

Method: 1. 从五个Reddit社区收集求助叙述，分解为有序片段逐轮呈现给语言模型；2. 使用社交支持行为代码（SSBC）这一已建立的多标签分类法对每个响应进行编码；3. 使用线性探针对隐藏表示进行编码，在不改变生成上下文的情况下估计用户苦恼的内部信号；4. 在两个中等规模模型（Llama-3.1-8B, OLMo-3-7B）上测试了超过6,200轮交互。

Result: 1. 支持策略随估计苦恼值发生系统性变化：随着估计苦恼上升，教学类支持策略下降（这一发现在不同架构中可复现）；2. 情感和自尊导向策略（如验证）的增加具有暗示性，但模型特异性较强且依赖于噪声较大的注释；3. 社区上下文独立影响行为模式，主要跟踪主题和话语规范而非人口统计类别。

Conclusion: 单轮评估无法捕捉到的轨迹级动态变化表明，需要为社交敏感应用开发多轮审计框架。研究揭示了语言模型支持策略随用户苦恼变化的系统性模式，这对社交支持机器人的设计和评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: When users seek social support from chatbots, they disclose their situation gradually, yet most evaluations of supportive LLMs rely on single-turn, fully specified prompts. We introduce a multi-turn simulation framework that closes this gap. Support-seeking narratives from five Reddit communities are decomposed into ordered fragments and revealed turn by turn to a language model. Each response is coded with the Social Support Behavior Code (SSBC), an established multi-label taxonomy that captures the composition of support, rather than a single quality score. To ask whether support choices track the model's own construal of user distress, we use linear probes on hidden representations to estimate this internal signal without altering the generation context. Across two mid-scale models (Llama-3.1-8B, OLMo-3-7B) and more than 6,200 turns, support composition shifts systematically with estimated distress: teaching declines as estimated distress rises, a finding that replicates across architectures, while increases in affective and esteem-oriented strategies (such as validation) are suggestive but model-specific and rest on noisier annotations. Community context independently shapes behavior, tracking topic and discourse norms rather than demographic categories. These trajectory-level dynamics, invisible to single-turn evaluation, motivate multi-turn auditing frameworks for socially sensitive applications.

</details>


### [91] [Comparing Human and Large Language Model Interpretation of Implicit Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17085)
*Antonio De Santis,Tommaso Bonetti,Andrea Tocchetti,Marco Brambilla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型的隐式信息提取（IIE）方法，通过构建结构化知识图谱来提取关系三元组、验证隐式推理和分析时序关系，发现LLM在隐式信息提取方面存在局限性，且与人类在处理不同类型上下文时的保守程度存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式意义解释是人类交流的重要组成部分，但这种框架可能无法直接应用于大语言模型（LLMs）的交互。研究旨在探索LLMs在隐式信息理解和提取方面的能力局限性。

Method: 提出了隐式信息提取（IIE）任务和基于LLM的IIE流程，该流程包括：从上下文句子中提取关系三元组、验证隐式推理、分析时序关系以构建结构化知识图谱。在两个数据集上评估了两个LLM与众包人类判断的对比。

Result: 人类认可大多数模型生成的三元组，但一致提出许多补充，表明当前基于LLM的IIE覆盖率有限。模型在社交丰富语境中对隐式推理比人类更保守，而人类在简短的事实导向语境中变得更保守。

Conclusion: 当前基于大语言模型的隐式信息提取方法仍存在局限性，模型与人类在处理隐式信息方面存在系统性差异，需要进一步改进以更好地理解和提取隐式含义。

Abstract: The interpretation of implicit meanings is an integral aspect of human communication. However, this framework may not transfer to interactions with Large Language Models (LLMs). To investigate this, we introduce the task of Implicit Information Extraction (IIE) and propose an LLM-based IIE pipeline that builds a structured knowledge graph from a context sentence by extracting relational triplets, validating implicit inferences, and analyzing temporal relations. We evaluate two LLMs against crowdsourced human judgments on two datasets. We find that humans agree with most model triplets yet consistently propose many additions, indicating limited coverage in current LLM-based IIE. Moreover, in our experiments, models appear to be more conservative about implicit inferences than humans in socially rich contexts, whereas humans become more conservative in shorter, fact-oriented contexts. Our code is available at https://github.com/Antonio-Dee/IIE_from_LLM.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Semantic Consensus: Process-Aware Conflict Detection and Resolution for Enterprise Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16339)
*Vivek Acharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了语义共识框架(SCF)，通过解决多智能体LLM系统中的语义意图分歧问题，实现100%工作流完成率，显著提升企业自动化系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI自动化中多智能体LLM系统的故障率高达41%-86.7%，其中约79%的失败源于规范和协调问题而非模型能力限制。语义意图分歧——由于隔离的上下文和缺失的流程模型导致协作LLM智能体对共享目标产生不一致解释——被认为是多智能体故障的主要但未被正式解决的根源。

Method: 提出了语义共识框架(SCF)，一个流程感知的中间件，包含六个组件：1) 用于共享操作语义的流程上下文层；2) 用于形式化意图表示的语义意图图；3) 用于实时识别矛盾、基于竞争和因果无效意图组合的冲突检测引擎；4) 使用策略-权威-时间层级的共识解决协议；5) 用于检测逐渐语义分歧的漂移监控器；6) 用于组织策略执行的流程感知治理集成层。

Result: 在三个多智能体框架(AutoGen、CrewAI、LangGraph)和四个企业场景的600次运行评估中，SCF是唯一实现100%工作流完成率的方法，而次优基线只有25.1%。SCF能检测65.2%的语义冲突，精确率为27.9%，并提供完整的治理审计追踪。框架是协议无关的，兼容MCP和A2A通信标准。

Conclusion: 语义共识框架(SCF)有效解决了多智能体LLM系统中的语义意图分歧问题，通过形式化的流程感知中间件设计，显著提升了企业自动化系统的可靠性和治理能力，为实现可扩展、可审计的多智能体AI自动化提供了系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems are rapidly emerging as the dominant architecture for enterprise AI automation, yet production deployments exhibit failure rates between 41% and 86.7%, with nearly 79% of failures originating from specification and coordination issues rather than model capability limitations. This paper identifies Semantic Intent Divergence--the phenomenon whereby cooperating LLM agents develop inconsistent interpretations of shared objectives due to siloed context and absent process models--as a primary yet formally unaddressed root cause of multi-agent failure in enterprise settings. We propose the Semantic Consensus Framework (SCF), a process-aware middleware comprising six components: a Process Context Layer for shared operational semantics, a Semantic Intent Graph for formal intent representation, a Conflict Detection Engine for real-time identification of contradictory, contention-based, and causally invalid intent combinations, a Consensus Resolution Protocol using a policy--authority--temporal hierarchy, a Drift Monitor for detecting gradual semantic divergence, and a Process-Aware Governance Integration layer for organizational policy enforcement. Evaluation across 600 runs spanning three multi-agent frameworks (AutoGen, CrewAI, LangGraph) and four enterprise scenarios demonstrates that SCF is the only approach to achieve 100% workflow completion--compared to 25.1% for the next-best baseline--while detecting 65.2% of semantic conflicts with 27.9% precision and providing complete governance audit trails. The framework is protocol-agnostic and compatible with MCP and A2A communication standards.

</details>


### [93] [Computational Hermeneutics: Evaluating generative AI as a cultural technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16403)
*Cody Kommers,Ruth Ahnert,Maria Antoniak,Emmanouil Benetos,Steve Benford,Mercedes Bunz,Baptiste Caramiaux,Shauna Concannon,Martin Disley,James Dobson,Yali Du,Edgar Duéñez-Guzmán,Kerry Francksen,Evelyn Gius,Jonathan W. Y. Gray,Ryan Heuser,Sarah Immel,Richard Jean So,Sang Leigh,Dalaki Livingston,Hoyt Long,Meredith Martin,Georgia Meyer,Daniela Mihai,Ashley Noel-Hirst,Kirsten Ostherr,Deven Parker,Yipeng Qin,Jessica Ratcliff,Emily Robinson,Karina Rodriguez,Adam Sobey,Ted Underwood,Aditya Vashistha,Matthew Wilkens,Youyou Wu,Yuan Zheng,Drew Hemment*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出计算阐释学作为评估生成式AI的新框架，认为AI系统应被视为"语境机器"而非单纯的信息处理器，强调对文化意义的语境化评估而非标准化准确性测试


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估框架将文化视为可测量的变量，而非系统运作的基本要素，这忽视了AI作为文化技术的本质。需要一种能够处理意义、阐释和文化语境的理论框架

Method: 基于人文学科的阐释学理论，提出AI系统应被视为"语境机器"，必须处理三个阐释挑战：情境性（意义只在语境中出现）、多元性（多种有效阐释共存）和模糊性（阐释自然冲突）。提出计算阐释学框架和三个评估原则

Result: 提出了三个阐释学评估原则：1)基准测试应是迭代的而非一次性；2)应包括人而不仅仅是机器；3)应测量文化语境而不仅仅是模型输出。形成了评估当代AI系统的崭新范式

Conclusion: 计算阐释学提供了一个新兴框架，用于解释生成式AI系统的作用及其改进方式。这一视角将从关于准确性的标准化问题转向关于意义的语境化问题，为AI系统的设计和评估提供了新范式

Abstract: Generative AI systems are increasingly recognized as cultural technologies, yet current evaluation frameworks often treat culture as a variable to be measured rather than fundamental to the system's operation. Drawing on hermeneutic theory from the humanities, we argue that GenAI systems function as "context machines" that must inherently address three interpretive challenges: situatedness (meaning only emerges in context), plurality (multiple valid interpretations coexist), and ambiguity (interpretations naturally conflict). We present computational hermeneutics as an emerging framework offering an interpretive account of what GenAI systems do, and how they might do it better. We offer three principles for hermeneutic evaluation -- that benchmarks should be iterative, not one-off; include people, not just machines; and measure cultural context, not just model output. This perspective offers a nascent paradigm for designing and evaluating contemporary AI systems: shifting from standardized questions about accuracy to contextual ones about meaning.

</details>


### [94] [Heterogeneous Self-Play for Realistic Highway Traffic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16406)
*Jinkai Qiu,Alessandro Saviolo,Chaojie Wang,Mingke Wang,Xiaoyu Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PHASE是一个用于高速公路场景生成的自博弈框架，通过异构智能体自博弈实现可控、广泛覆盖且行为可信的高速公路交通仿真，在未见过的真实场景中实现了零样本迁移和性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的高速公路仿真对于自动驾驶车辆的安全评估至关重要，但现有方法面临三大挑战：1）需要在速度和机动操作上具有广泛覆盖性；2）需要可控生成罕见的安全关键场景；3）需要在多智能体交互中保持行为可信度。目前的交通生成方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: PHASE（Policy for Heterogeneous Agent Self-play on Expressway）采用基于上下文的自博弈框架：1）通过显式的每智能体条件实现可控性；2）利用合成场景生成实现广泛的高速公路覆盖；3）通过闭环多智能体训练确保真实的交互动态。框架支持不同车辆类型（如轿车和挂车），通过车辆感知动态和上下文条件动作实现。采用多种稳定化技术：不可恢复状态的早期终止、碰撞责任归属、高速公路感知奖励塑形、耦合课程学习和鲁棒策略优化。

Result: 在仅使用合成数据训练的情况下，PHASE能够零样本迁移到512个未见过的高交互真实场景（exiD数据集），成功率达到96.3%，与之前自博弈基线相比，平均位移误差（ADE）从6.57米降至2.44米，最终位移误差（FDE）从12.07米降至5.25米。在学习的轨迹嵌入空间中，相对于IDM模型，将Fréchet轨迹距离降低了13.1%，能量距离降低了20.2%，显著提升了行为真实性。

Conclusion: 合成自博弈提供了一种可扩展的途径，能够实现可控且真实的高速公路场景生成，无需直接模仿专家数据。PHASE框架成功解决了高速公路交通生成的三大核心挑战，为零样本泛化和安全评估提供了有效的仿真工具。

Abstract: Realistic highway simulation is critical for scalable safety evaluation of autonomous vehicles, particularly for interactions that are too rare to study from logged data alone. Yet highway traffic generation remains challenging because it requires broad coverage across speeds and maneuvers, controllable generation of rare safety-critical scenarios, and behavioral credibility in multi-agent interactions. We present PHASE, Policy for Heterogeneous Agent Self-play on Expressway, a context-aware self-play framework that addresses these three requirements through explicit per-agent conditioning for controllability, synthetic scenario generation for broad highway coverage, and closed-loop multi-agent training for realistic interaction dynamics. PHASE further supports different vehicle profiles, for example, passenger cars and articulated trailer trucks, within a single policy via vehicle-aware dynamics and context-conditioned actions, and stabilizes self-play with early termination of unrecoverable states, at-fault collision attribution, highway-aware reward shaping, coupled curricula, and robust policy optimization. Despite being trained only on synthetic data, PHASE transfers zero-shot to 512 unseen high-interaction real scenarios in exiD, achieving a 96.3% success rate and reducing ADE/FDE from 6.57/12.07 m to 2.44/5.25 m relative to a prior self-play baseline. In a learned trajectory embedding space, it also improves behavioral realism over IDM, reducing Frechet trajectory distance by 13.1% and energy distance by 20.2%. These results show that synthetic self-play can provide a scalable route to controllable and realistic highway scenario generation without direct imitation of expert logs.

</details>


### [95] [Support Sufficiency as Consequence-Sensitive Compression in Belief Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16434)
*Mark Walsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持感知的适应性控制架构，通过动态调整证据保留程度来平衡表征精度与计算资源，优于固定分辨率的控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准假设选择模型假设选定内容和标量置信度足以支持下游控制，但本文认为这不够。证据压缩会导致重要结构信息丢失，而确定哪些信息必须在压缩中保留本身就是一个后果敏感的问题。

Method: 开发了一种递归仲裁架构，其中活动约束场共同确定候选假设的几何结构。系统不完整保留此几何结构，而是将其压缩为支持感知的控制状态，该状态的解析度由当前后果几何、仲裁记忆和资源约束共同调节。

Result: 1）太少的支持保留会导致策略相关区分崩溃，产生虽然内容选择充分但错误路由验证、弃权和恢复的控制器。2）过多的支持保留会在过于精细的上下文中分散学习，即使辨别力提高也会降低适应性。3）适应性控制器（调节支持解析度）在累积效用上优于所有固定解析度控制器。4）敏捷适应性控制优于迟缓适应性控制。5）固定高解析度控制获得最佳承诺准确率，但因资源成本和学习分散而仍落后于适应性控制器。

Conclusion: 支持充分性不应被视为静态表征阈值，而应是动态压缩标准。鲁棒仲裁依赖于在当前后果景观下保留对策略足够的最小支持结构，并在推理和行动的重复周期中随着条件变化调节该结构。

Abstract: When a system commits to a hypothesis, much of the evidential structure behind that commitment is lost to compression. Standard accounts assume that selected content and scalar confidence suffice for downstream control. This paper argues that they do not, and that determining what must survive compression is itself a consequence-sensitive problem. We develop a recurrent arbitration architecture in which active constraint fields jointly determine a hypothesis geometry over candidates. Rather than carrying that geometry forward in full, the system compresses it into a support-aware control state whose resolution is regulated by current consequence geometry, arbitration memory, and resource constraints.
  A bounded objective formalizes the tradeoff. Too little retained support collapses policy-relevant distinctions, producing controllers that select content adequately while misrouting verification, abstention, and recovery. Too much retained support fragments learning across overly fine contexts, degrading adaptation even as discrimination improves. These failure modes yield ordered controller predictions confirmed by a minimal repeated-interaction simulation. Adaptive controllers that regulate support resolution outperform all fixed-resolution controllers in cumulative utility. Agile adaptive control outperforms sluggish adaptive control. Fixed high-resolution control achieves the best commitment accuracy but still trails adaptive controllers because resource cost and learning fragmentation offset the gains from richer retention.
  Support sufficiency should be understood not as a static representational threshold, but as a dynamic compression criterion. Robust arbitration depends on preserving the smallest support structure adequate for policy under the current consequence landscape, and on regulating that structure as conditions change across repeated cycles of inference and action.

</details>


### [96] [Healthcare AI for Automation or Allocation? A Transaction Cost Economics Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16465)
*Ari Ercole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 医疗保健生产力不仅受临床复杂性影响，还受到不确定性下的工作协调成本影响。研究通过任务级分析发现，临床角色比非临床角色具有更高的交易成本强度，主要源于信息搜索和决策相关协调负担，这影响了数字化和AI干预的分布机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗保健生产力受协调工作成本影响，交易成本经济学为此提供了理论基础，但很少在医疗职业的任务层面进行操作化。需要理解医疗工作中不同类型协调摩擦的分布模式，以及这些协调结构如何影响技术干预的机会。

Method: 使用O*NET职业数据库中的任务陈述和频率权重，在任务粒度上表征医疗保健工作。通过约束性大型语言模型将每个独特任务编码到一个主导交易成本类别（信息搜索、决策和谈判、监督和执行、适应和协调），并给出总体交易成本强度分数。最后将结果聚合到职业层面进行分析。

Result: 临床角色表现出显著高于非临床角色的交易成本强度，主要驱动因素是更大的信息搜索和决策相关协调负担。职业内部的交易成本分散度没有显著差异。这些发现表明协调工作的性质在不同医疗角色中存在系统性异质性。

Conclusion: 数字化和AI干预的机会分布不均，受基础协调结构影响更多于技术任务复杂性。理解交易成本强度模式有助于更有效地部署技术解决方案，优先解决协调负担最重的临床工作领域。

Abstract: Healthcare productivity is shaped not only by clinical complexity but by the costs of coordinating work under uncertainty. Transaction-cost economics offers a theory of these coordination frictions, yet has rarely been operationalised at task level across health occupations. Using task statements and frequency weights from the O*NET occupational database, we characterised healthcare work at task granularity and coded each unique task using a constrained large language model into one dominant transaction-cost category (information search, decision and bargaining, monitoring and enforcement, or adaptation and coordination) together with an overall transaction-cost intensity score. Aggregating to the occupation level, clinician roles exhibited substantially higher transaction-cost intensity than non-clinician roles, driven primarily by greater burdens of information search and decision-related coordination, while dispersion of transaction costs within occupations did not differ. These findings demonstrate systematic heterogeneity in the nature of coordination work across healthcare roles and suggest that the opportunities for digital and AI interventions are unevenly distributed, shaped less by technical task complexity than by underlying coordination structure.

</details>


### [97] [From Subsumption to Satisfiability: LLM-Assisted Active Learning for OWL Ontologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16672)
*Haoruo Zhao,Wenshuo Tang,Duncan Guthrie,Michele Sevegnani,David Flynn,Paul Harvey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在主动学习中利用大语言模型（LLMs）处理成员查询（MQs）的新方法，通过将子类关系测试转化为反概念的可满足性问题，并使用受控自然语言向LLMs提问来获取现实世界实例，从而确保在知识本体建模中只可能出现第二类错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的知识本体学习中，成员查询需要依赖人工教师来回答问题，如"每个苹果都是水果吗？"。作者受到描述逻辑中子类关系测试可归约为可满足性问题的启发，探索如何利用LLMs作为近似现实世界知识源的替代方案，以减少人工参与并提高效率。

Method: 1. 将每个候选公理转化为其对应的反概念；2. 使用受控自然语言将反概念表达为LLMs可理解的问题；3. 引入LLMs作为第三组件，提供近似反概念实例的现实世界例子；4. 通过这种方式设计确保在知识本体建模中只可能出现第二类错误。

Result: 在13个商业LLMs上的实验结果显示，该方法在各种已建立的知识本体中保持稳定的召回率（对应于框架中的第二类错误）。这意味着第二类错误率保持相对恒定，不会因知识本体不同而产生显著变化。

Conclusion: 该方法通过巧妙地将LLMs集成到主动学习框架中，既利用了LLMs的现实世界知识能力，又通过设计保证只产生第二类错误，从而避免了知识本体不一致的风险。最坏情况下，第二类错误只会延迟构建过程而不会引入矛盾，为知识本体学习提供了一种安全有效的自动化途径。

Abstract: In active learning, membership queries (MQs) allow a learner to pose questions to a teacher, such as ''Is every apple a fruit?'', to which the teacher responds correctly with yes or no. These MQs can be viewed as subsumption tests with respect to the target ontology. Inspired by the standard reduction of subsumption to satisfiability in description logics, we reformulate each candidate axiom into its corresponding counter-concept and verbalise it in controlled natural language before presenting it to Large Language Models (LLMs). We introduce LLMs as a third component that provides real-world examples approximating an instance of the counter-concept. This design property ensures that only Type II errors may occur in ontology modelling; in the worst case, these errors merely delay the construction process without introducing inconsistencies. Experimental results on 13 commercial LLMs show that recall, corresponding to Type II errors in our framework, remains stable across several well-established ontologies.

</details>


### [98] [Agentic Risk-Aware Set-Based Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16687)
*Varun Kumar,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的多智能体框架，采用人在环范式辅助空气动力学翼型早期设计，整合集合设计和风险量化，通过自动化探索筛选风险调整的候选设计方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计的早期阶段通常面临巨大的参数空间和不确定性，需要系统化的方法探索设计选项并管理风险。传统方法中高计算负荷的初始探索和分析任务需要自动化，以增强人类专家的决策能力

Method: 1. 基于LLM的多智能体框架：编码助手、设计智能体、系统工程智能体、分析师智能体，由人类管理者协调
2. 采用集合设计理念：先协作开发验证工具，再执行结构化工作流
3. 集成正式风险管理：使用条件风险价值(CVaR)作为量化指标筛选设计
4. 自动化初始探索：分析师智能体执行全局灵敏度分析生成启发性规则

Result: 框架成功应用于空气动力学翼型设计，能够系统化探索和修剪大量初始设计候选。通过自动化处理高容量分析任务，为人类管理者提供经过风险量化和高保真CFD模拟验证的精选候选设计集，增强专家在最终风险评估设计选择中的决策能力

Conclusion: 该多智能体框架有效整合了AI自动化分析和人类专家判断，通过在工程设计早期阶段引入系统化的风险管理和集合设计方法，显著提高了设计探索的效率和决策质量。特别是CVaR的集成提供了一种量化风险的方法，使设计选择更加科学和可靠

Abstract: This paper introduces a multi-agent framework guided by Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist in the early stages of engineering design, a phase often characterized by vast parameter spaces and inherent uncertainty. Operating under a human-in-the-loop paradigm and demonstrated on the canonical problem of aerodynamic airfoil design, the framework employs a team of specialized agents: a Coding Assistant, a Design Agent, a Systems Engineering Agent, and an Analyst Agent - all coordinated by a human Manager. Integrated within a set-based design philosophy, the process begins with a collaborative phase where the Manager and Coding Assistant develop a suite of validated tools, after which the agents execute a structured workflow to systematically explore and prune a large set of initial design candidates. A key contribution of this work is the explicit integration of formal risk management, employing the Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) as a quantitative metric to filter designs that exhibit a high probability of failing to meet performance requirements, specifically the target coefficient of lift. The framework automates labor-intensive initial exploration through a global sensitivity analysis conducted by the Analyst agent, which generates actionable heuristics to guide the other agents. The process culminates by presenting the human Manager with a curated final set of promising design candidates, augmented with high-fidelity Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations. This approach effectively leverages AI to handle high-volume analytical tasks, thereby enhancing the decision-making capability of the human expert in selecting the final, risk-assessed design.

</details>


### [99] [The Query Channel: Information-Theoretic Limits of Masking-Based Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16689)
*Erciyes Karakaya,Ozgur Ercetin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个信息论框架，将基于掩码的后验解释方法（如KernelSHAP和LIME）建模为通信信道，其中解释复杂度由假设类的熵表征，查询接口提供信息速率由每查询识别容量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于掩码的解释方法（如KernelSHAP和LIME）缺乏严格的理论基础来理解其性能限制和可靠性。需要建立信息论框架来量化解释的可恢复性、查询复杂度与解释质量之间的关系。

Method: 1. 将解释过程建模为通信信道：潜在解释作为消息，每个掩码评估作为信道使用；2. 推导强逆定理证明当解释速率超过容量时，精确恢复概率必然趋于错误；3. 证明稀疏最大似然解码器在速率低于容量时可实现可靠恢复；4. 提出互信息的蒙特卡洛估计器作为非渐近查询基准；5. 通过实验比较最优解码与Lasso/OLS过程（对应LIME和KernelSHAP）；6. 分析超像素分辨率、token化、高斯噪声和非线性曲率对信道的影响。

Result: 1. 建立了基于掩码解释方法的信息论框架；2. 证明了强逆定理和可实现性定理；3. 识别出存在查询预算范围，其中信息论允许可靠解释但标准凸替代方法失败；4. 发现超像素分辨率/token化作为设定解释熵的信源编码选择；5. 证明了高斯噪声和非线性曲率会降低查询信道质量，导致瀑布和误差平台行为，使高分辨率解释不可实现。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一个信息论框架来分析基于掩码的解释方法，揭示了其性能基本限制，解释了为什么某些解释任务在当前方法下不可行，并为设计更可靠的解释器提供了理论基础和指导。

Abstract: Masking-based post-hoc explanation methods, such as KernelSHAP and LIME, estimate local feature importance by querying a black-box model under randomized perturbations. This paper formulates this procedure as communication over a query channel, where the latent explanation acts as a message and each masked evaluation is a channel use. Within this framework, the complexity of the explanation is captured by the entropy of the hypothesis class, while the query interface supplies information at a rate determined by an identification capacity per query. We derive a strong converse showing that, if the explanation rate exceeds this capacity, the probability of exact recovery necessarily converges to one in error for any sequence of explainers and decoders. We also prove an achievability result establishing that a sparse maximum-likelihood decoder attains reliable recovery when the rate lies below capacity. A Monte Carlo estimator of mutual information yields a non-asymptotic query benchmark that we use to compare optimal decoding with Lasso- and OLS-based procedures that mirror LIME and KernelSHAP. Experiments reveal a range of query budgets where information theory permits reliable explanations but standard convex surrogates still fail. Finally, we interpret super-pixel resolution and tokenization for neural language models as a source-coding choice that sets the entropy of the explanation and show how Gaussian noise and nonlinear curvature degrade the query channel, induce waterfall and error-floor behavior, and render high-resolution explanations unattainable.

</details>


### [100] [RankGuide: Tensor-Rank-Guided Routing and Steering for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16694)
*Jiayi Tian,Yupeng Su,Ryan Solgi,Souvik Kundu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RankGuide是一个通过张量秩引导的路由和调控来改进小型推理模型与大型推理模型协作的框架，能够在减少1.75倍延迟的同时保持竞争性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型生成多步思维链推理虽然提升了问题解决能力，但带来了显著推理延迟和计算开销。小型推理模型协作可以更好权衡准确率与延迟，但如何有效检测和缓解小型推理模型的失败仍是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出RankGuide框架，在生成文本和隐藏状态空间分析小型推理模型推理过程，识别三种失败模式（过度自信、不确定性、重复验证）。利用连续隐藏状态导出的张量秩信号构建路由信号，检测小型推理模型可能失败时机并选择性调用大型推理模型。同时引入张量秩过滤的调控向量提取方法，调控小型推理模型的推理轨迹，提高生成质量。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试中，RankGuide相比大型推理模型能减少高达1.75倍的延迟，同时相对于先前方法保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: 通过张量秩信号改进路由和调控，RankGuide使小型推理模型与大型推理模型协作系统能够以更少步骤和更高准确率实现更高效的推理。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) enhance problem-solving capabilities by generating explicit multi-step chains of thought (CoT) reasoning; however, they incur substantial inference latency and computational overhead. To mitigate this issue, recent works have explored model collaboration paradigms, where small reasoning models (SRMs) generate intermediate reasoning steps to achieve a better accuracy--latency trade-off. Despite recent progress, effectively and efficiently detecting and mitigating SRM failures in collaborative systems remains a key challenge. To address this issue, we analyze SRM inference in both the generated text and hidden-state spaces, and identify three types of failure modes: \textit{overconfidence}, \textit{uncertainty}, and \textit{heavy revalidation}. Building on these insights, we propose \textbf{RankGuide}, a framework that improves the efficiency and effectiveness of SRM--LRM collaboration through tensor-rank-guided routing and steering. Specifically, RankGuide leverages a routing signal that incorporates tensor-rank signals derived from consecutive hidden states to detect when SRMs are likely to fail and selectively invoke LRMs. In addition, we introduce a tensor-rank-filtered steering vector extraction method to modulate the reasoning trajectory of SRMs, thereby improving their generation quality. By improving both routing and steering through tensor-rank signals, RankGuide enables SRM--LRM collaborative systems to achieve more efficient reasoning with fewer steps and improved accuracy. Experiments on multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the efficacy of RankGuide in reducing latency by up to $1.75\times$ compared to LRM, while maintaining competitive accuracy relative to prior methods.

</details>


### [101] [Know When to Trust the Skill: Delayed Appraisal and Epistemic Vigilance for Single-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16753)
*Eren Unlu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MESA-S框架，通过将标量置信度估计转换为向量（分离自我置信度与源置信度），并引入延迟程序探针机制和元认知技能卡，来缓解上下文污染和"过度思考"问题，实现更可靠的单智能体编排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型转变为集成广泛工具生态系统的自主智能体，传统路由启发式方法日益受到上下文污染和"过度思考"问题困扰。瓶颈不在于算法能力或技能多样性的缺乏，而在于缺乏严格的二阶元认知治理。

Method: 引入MESA-S（元认知技能智能体，单智能体）框架，将标量置信度估计转换为分离自我置信度（参数确定性）和源置信度（对外部检索程序的信任）的向量。通过形式化延迟程序探针机制和引入元认知技能卡，将技能效用意识与其令牌密集型执行解耦。

Result: 在通过Gemini 3.1 Pro原生执行的上下文静态基准评估中，早期结果表明，显式编程信任来源和延迟升级能够缓解供应链漏洞、修剪不必要的推理循环，并防止卸载引起的置信度膨胀。

Conclusion: 该架构提供了科学谨慎、行为锚定的步骤，朝着可靠、认识论警惕的单智能体编排方向发展，为解决自主智能体中的元认知治理问题提供了初步框架。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) transition into autonomous agents integrated with extensive tool ecosystems, traditional routing heuristics increasingly succumb to context pollution and "overthinking". We argue that the bottleneck is not a deficit in algorithmic capability or skill diversity, but the absence of disciplined second-order metacognitive governance. In this paper, our scientific contribution focuses on the computational translation of human cognitive control - specifically, delayed appraisal, epistemic vigilance, and region-of-proximal offloading - into a single-agent architecture. We introduce MESA-S (Metacognitive Skills for Agents, Single-agent), a preliminary framework that shifts scalar confidence estimation into a vector separating self-confidence (parametric certainty) from source-confidence (trust in retrieved external procedures). By formalizing a delayed procedural probe mechanism and introducing Metacognitive Skill Cards, MESA-S decouples the awareness of a skill's utility from its token-intensive execution. Evaluated under an In-Context Static Benchmark Evaluation natively executed via Gemini 3.1 Pro, our early results suggest that explicitly programming trust provenance and delayed escalation mitigates supply-chain vulnerabilities, prunes unnecessary reasoning loops, and prevents offloading-induced confidence inflation. This architecture offers a scientifically cautious, behaviorally anchored step toward reliable, epistemically vigilant single-agent orchestration.

</details>


### [102] [Machine individuality: Separating genuine idiosyncrasy from response bias in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16755)
*Valentin Kriegmair,Dirk U. Wulff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用交叉随机效应模型分析大型语言模型的个体差异，发现LLMs具有稳定的刺激特异性个体性，这种"机器个体性"占方差16.9%，超越了统计零模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在日常生活中越来越广泛的应用（从高风险决策支持到陪伴），理解它们的行为倾向变得至关重要。虽然已有文献使用心理测量量表和认知范式来分析LLM倾向，但这些方法无法确定行为差异是反映稳定的、刺激特定的个体性，还是全局响应偏差和随机噪声。

Method: 应用交叉随机效应模型（心理测量学中广泛用于分离系统性效应的技术），分析10个开源大型语言模型对超过100,000个单词在14项心理语言学规范中提供的7490万个评分数据。

Result: 平均16.9%的方差归因于刺激特异性个体性，稳健地超过了统计零模型。跨规范预测分析显示这种个体性是一个连贯的"指纹"，每个模型都具有独特性。这些差异不能归因于响应偏差或随机噪声。

Conclusion: 研究识别了大型语言模型之间的个体差异，这些差异不能归因于响应偏差或随机噪声。研究者将这些差异称为"机器个体性"，为理解LLM的稳定行为倾向提供了新框架。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into daily life, in roles ranging from high-stakes decision support to companionship, understanding their behavioral dispositions becomes critical. A growing literature uses psychometric inventories and cognitive paradigms to profile LLM dispositions. However, these approaches cannot determine whether behavioral differences reflect stable, stimulus-specific individuality or global response biases and stochastic noise. Here, we apply crossed random-effects models -- widely used in psychometrics to separate systematic effects -- to 74.9 million ratings provided by 10 open-weight LLMs for over 100,000 words across 14 psycholinguistic norms. On average, 16.9% of variance is attributable to stimulus-specific individuality, robustly exceeding a statistical null model. Cross-norm prediction analyses reveal this individuality as a coherent fingerprint, unique to each model. These results identify individual differences among LLMs that cannot be attributed to response biases or stochastic noise. We term these differences machine individuality.

</details>


### [103] [SAVE: A Generalizable Framework for Multi-Condition Single-Cell Generation with Gene Block Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16776)
*Jiahao Li,Jiayi Dong,Peng Ye,Xiaochi Zhou,Haohai Lu,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAVE是一个基于条件Transformer的单细胞多条件建模统一生成框架，通过将语义相关基因分组为块来捕获高阶依赖关系，在生成保真度和外推泛化方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的单细胞基因表达建模方法通常将基因视为独立标记，忽视了基因间的高层生物关系，导致性能不佳。需要一种能够跨多样生物和技术条件建模的方法来表征细胞状态并模拟未见场景。

Method: SAVE采用基于条件Transformer的统一生成框架，通过粗粒度表示将语义相关基因分组为块以捕获基因模块间的高阶依赖关系。结合Flow Matching机制和条件掩码策略增强灵活模拟能力，并支持对未见条件组合的泛化。

Result: SAVE在条件生成、批次效应校正和扰动预测等一系列基准测试中表现优异，在生成保真度和外推泛化方面持续优于最先进方法，特别是在低资源或组合保留设置下表现突出。

Conclusion: SAVE为复杂单细胞数据建模提供了一个可扩展且可泛化的解决方案，在虚拟细胞合成和生物解释方面具有广泛实用性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Modeling single-cell gene expression across diverse biological and technical conditions is crucial for characterizing cellular states and simulating unseen scenarios. Existing methods often treat genes as independent tokens, overlooking their high-level biological relationships and leading to poor performance. We introduce SAVE, a unified generative framework based on conditional Transformers for multi-condition single-cell modeling. SAVE leverages a coarse-grained representation by grouping semantically related genes into blocks, capturing higher-order dependencies among gene modules. A Flow Matching mechanism and condition-masking strategy further enhance flexible simulation and enable generalization to unseen condition combinations. We evaluate SAVE on a range of benchmarks, including conditional generation, batch effect correction, and perturbation prediction. SAVE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in generation fidelity and extrapolative generalization, especially in low-resource or combinatorially held-out settings. Overall, SAVE offers a scalable and generalizable solution for modeling complex single-cell data, with broad utility in virtual cell synthesis and biological interpretation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/fdu-wangfeilab/sc-save

</details>


### [104] [Introspection Adapters: Training LLMs to Report Their Learned Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16812)
*Keshav Shenoy,Li Yang,Abhay Sheshadri,Sören Mindermann,Jack Lindsey,Sam Marks,Rowan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种称为"自省适配器（IA）"的可扩展方法来审核微调后的LLMs，该方法通过在训练数据中植入行为来训练LoRA适配器，使模型能够用自然语言描述自身学习到的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当开发人员或用户微调大型语言模型（LLMs）时，可能会引入意外的、有害的或难以检测的行为。当前缺乏一种快速、可扩展的方法来审核多个基于相同基础模型微调的LLMs。

Method: 1. 从基础模型M出发，微调多个模型Mi，并为每个模型植入特定行为bi；2. 使用这些(Mi, bi)对作为有标签的训练数据；3. 训练一个"自省适配器（IA）"——这是一个在多个微调模型Mi上联合训练的单一LoRA适配器，能够使模型口头描述其植入的行为；4. 该方法在不同训练方式下微调的模型上也能有效工作。

Result: 1. IA能够诱导模型自我描述学习到的行为，即使是在与训练数据中不同方式微调的模型上；2. 在AuditBench基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，能够识别明确隐藏的令人担忧的行为；3. 可以用于检测加密微调API攻击；4. 随着模型规模和训练数据多样性的增加，性能可扩展性良好。

Conclusion: 自省适配器（IAs）是一种可扩展、有效且实用的方法来审核微调后的LLMs，为解决LLM行为审计问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: When model developers or users fine-tune an LLM, this can induce behaviors that are unexpected, deliberately harmful, or hard to detect. It would be far easier to audit LLMs if they could simply describe their behaviors in natural language. Here, we study a scalable approach to rapidly identify learned behaviors of many LLMs derived from a shared base LLM. Given a model $M$, our method works by finetuning models $M_i$ from $M$ with implanted behaviors $b_i$; the $(M_i, b_i)$ pairs serve as labeled training data. We then train an \emph{introspection adapter} (IA): a single LoRA adapter jointly trained across the finetunes $M_i$ to cause them to verbalize their implanted behaviors. We find that this IA induces self-description of learned behaviors even in finetunes of $M$ that were trained in very different ways from the $M_i$. For example, IAs generalize to AuditBench, achieving state-of-the-art at identifying explicitly hidden concerning behaviors. IAs can also be used to detect encrypted finetuning API attacks. They scale favorably with model size and training data diversity. Overall, our results suggest that IAs are a scalable, effective, and practically useful approach to auditing fine-tuned LLMs.

</details>


### [105] [Step-GRPO: Internalizing Dynamic Early Exit for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16890)
*Benteng Chen,Weida Wang,Shufei Zhang,Mingbao Lin,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Step-GRPO是一个后训练框架，通过语言标记结构化推理，让大型推理模型内部化动态早期退出能力，在保持准确性的同时显著减少计算冗余


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型使用长思维链在解决问题时表现出色，但存在计算冗余问题。现有的解决方案各有缺陷：训练时长度惩罚会损害模型能力，而推理时早期退出会增加系统开销。需要一种能平衡准确性和效率的解决方案。

Method: Step-GRPO将优化目标从原始标记转移到语义步骤，利用语言标记来结构化推理。引入动态截断推出机制，在探索期间向模型展示简洁的高置信度轨迹，并与步骤感知相对奖励协同工作，基于群体基线动态惩罚冗余。

Result: 在三个不同规模的模型上进行的广泛实验表明，Step-GRPO实现了优越的准确性-效率权衡。在Qwen3-8B模型上，该方法相比原始模型减少了32.0%的标记消耗，同时避免了传统长度惩罚方法中观察到的准确性下降。

Conclusion: Step-GRPO成功解决了大型推理模型的过度思考问题，通过在模型内部集成动态早期退出能力，实现了不牺牲准确性的显著效率提升，为推理模型的优化提供了新的后训练框架。

Abstract: Large reasoning models that use long chain-of-thought excel at problem-solving yet waste compute on redundant checks. Curbing this overthinking is hard: training-time length penalties can cripple ability, while inference-time early-exit adds system overhead. To bridge this gap, we propose Step-GRPO, a novel post-training framework that internalizes dynamic early-exit capabilities directly into the model. Step-GRPO shifts the optimization objective from raw tokens to semantic steps by utilizing linguistic markers to structure reasoning. We introduce a Dynamic Truncated Rollout mechanism that exposes the model to concise high-confidence trajectories during exploration, synergized with a Step-Aware Relative Reward that dynamically penalizes redundancy based on group-level baselines. Extensive experiments across three model sizes on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that Step-GRPO achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off. On Qwen3-8B, our method reduces token consumption by 32.0\% compared to the vanilla model while avoiding the accuracy degradation observed in traditional length-penalty methods.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond Text-Dominance: Understanding Modality Preference of Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16902)
*Xinru Yan,Boxi Cao,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Weixiang Zhou,Le Sun,Xianpei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文量化分析了大语言模型中的模态偏好现象，发现现代模型存在视觉偏好而非传统模型的文本主导，揭示了偏好形成的层间渐进特性，并提出利用内部信号诊断跨模态幻觉的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前原生多模态大语言模型已从流水线架构转向统一表示空间，但这种原生集成引发了一个关键但未被充分探索的现象：模态偏好。传统视觉-语言模型（VLMs）通常表现出"文本主导"特性，而现代OLLMs的模态偏好特性尚未系统研究，需要填补这一知识空白。

Method: 1. 使用新构建的基于冲突的基准和模态选择率指标系统量化OLLMs的模态偏好；2. 评估十个代表性OLLMs；3. 通过分层探测分析模态偏好的形成机制；4. 利用内部信号诊断跨模态幻觉，并在三个下游多模态基准上验证效果。

Result: 1. 发现了显著的范式转变：不同于传统VLMs的"文本主导"，大多数OLLMs表现出明显的视觉偏好；2. 模态偏好并非静态而是在中后期层逐步形成；3. 提出的跨模态幻觉诊断方法在三个多模态基准上取得了有竞争力的性能，无需任务特定数据。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统量化了OLLMs中的模态偏好现象，揭示了偏好形成的渐进机制，并开发了基于内部信号的跨模态幻觉诊断工具，为构建更可信的多模态大语言模型提供了机制理解和实践工具。

Abstract: Native Omni-modal Large Language Models (OLLMs) have shifted from pipeline architectures to unified representation spaces. However, this native integration gives rise to a critical yet underexplored phenomenon: modality preference. To bridge this gap, we first systematically quantify modality preference of OLLMs using a newly-curated conflict-based benchmark and the modality selection rate metric. Our evaluation of ten representative OLLMs reveals a notable paradigm shift: unlike the ``text-dominance'' of traditional VLMs, most OLLMs exhibit a pronounced visual preference. To further understand the underlying mechanism, we conduct layer-wise probing and demonstrate that such modality preference is not static but emerges progressively in the mid-to-late layers. Building upon these insights, we leverage these internal signals to diagnose cross-modal hallucinations, achieving competitive performance across three downstream multi-modal benchmarks without task-specific data. Our work provides both a mechanistic understanding and a practical tool for building more trustworthy OLLMs. Our code and related resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/icip-cas/OmniPreference

</details>


### [107] [Skilldex: A Package Manager and Registry for Agent Skill Packages with Hierarchical Scope-Based Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16911)
*Sampriti Saha,Pranav Hemanth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Skilldex是一个针对LLM智能体技能包的管理器和注册表系统，提供格式合规性评分和技能集捆绑功能，解决现有技能包管理中的两个关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体技能包管理存在两个主要问题：1) 缺乏对Anthropic格式规范的公开验证工具；2) 没有机制将相关技能与共享上下文捆绑以保持行为一致性。社区已有安装工具和注册表，但这些关键缺口尚未填补。

Method: Skilldex包含两个核心创新：1) 编译器风格的格式合规性评分系统，针对Anthropic技能规范提供行级诊断，包括描述特异性、前置元数据有效性和结构符合性检查；2) 技能集抽象概念，将相关技能与共享资产（词汇文件、模板、参考文档）捆绑，确保跨技能行为一致性。系统还提供三层分层范围系统、人机协同建议循环、仅元数据社区注册表和MCP服务器等基础设施支持。

Result: 系统以TypeScript CLI工具(skillpm/spm)形式实现，采用Hono/Supabase作为注册表后端，且已开源。Skilldex完整解决了技能包管理的两个关键缺口，提供了端到端的技能包管理解决方案。

Conclusion: Skilldex作为技能包管理器和注册表，通过格式验证和技能集捆绑机制，填补了LLM智能体运行时技能扩展的关键技术缺口，为技能包的质量控制和一致性维护提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly extended at runtime via skill packages, structured natural-language instruction bundles loaded from a well-known directory. Community install tooling and registries exist, but two gaps persist: no public tool scores skill packages against Anthropic's published format specification, and no mechanism bundles related skills with the shared context they need to remain mutually coherent. We present Skilldex, a package manager and registry for agent skill packages addressing both gaps. The two novel contributions are: (1) compiler-style format conformance scoring against Anthropic's skill specification, producing line-level diagnostics on description specificity, frontmatter validity, and structural adherence; and (2) the skillset abstraction, a bundled collection of related skills with shared assets (vocabulary files, templates, reference documents) that enforce cross-skill behavioral coherence. Skilldex also provides supporting infrastructure: a three-tier hierarchical scope system, a human-in-the-loop agent suggestion loop, a metadata-only community registry, and a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server. The system is implemented as a TypeScript CLI (skillpm / spm) with a Hono/Supabase registry backend, and is open-source.

</details>


### [108] [The Cognitive Penalty: Ablating System 1 and System 2 Reasoning in Edge-Native SLMs for Decentralized Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16913)
*Syed Muhammad Aqdas Rizvi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现对于边缘原生的小型语言模型在去中心化自治组织中，系统1（直觉推理）比系统2（深度推理）在对抗性环境中表现更优越，前者实现了100%对抗鲁棒性和更快的速度，而后者引入了推理不收敛和共识不稳定等严重问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化自治组织倾向于使用小型语言模型作为边缘原生宪法防火墙来审查提案和减轻语义社会工程攻击。虽然在推理时扩展计算（系统2）可以增强形式逻辑，但其在高对抗性的密码经济治理环境中的有效性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 引入了Sentinel-Bench，一个包含840次推理的实证框架，在Qwen-3.5-9B模型上执行严格的模型内消融实验。通过切换冻结权重上的潜在推理，隔离了推理时计算对对抗性Optimism DAO数据集的影响。

Result: 自回归基线（系统1）实现了100%对抗鲁棒性、100%司法一致性，并在13秒内完成状态最终确定。相反，系统2推理引入了灾难性不稳定性，主要由26.7%的推理不收敛率驱动。这导致试验间共识稳定性降至72.6%，并产生17倍的延迟开销，引入治理可提取价值和硬件集中化的关键漏洞。此外，实证捕获了1.5%对抗性试验中的"推理诱导的谄媚行为"，模型生成长达25,750字符的内部独白来合理化对抗性陷阱的失败。

Conclusion: 对于在拜占庭容错约束下运行的边缘原生小型语言模型，系统1参数化直觉在结构和经济上优于系统2迭代审议，适用于去中心化共识场景。

Abstract: Decentralized Autonomous Organizations (DAOs) are inclined explore Small Language Models (SLMs) as edge-native constitutional firewalls to vet proposals and mitigate semantic social engineering. While scaling inference-time compute (System 2) enhances formal logic, its efficacy in highly adversarial, cryptoeconomic governance environments remains underexplored. To address this, we introduce Sentinel-Bench, an 840-inference empirical framework executing a strict intra-model ablation on Qwen-3.5-9B. By toggling latent reasoning across frozen weights, we isolate the impact of inference-time compute against an adversarial Optimism DAO dataset. Our findings reveal a severe compute-accuracy inversion. The autoregressive baseline (System 1) achieved 100% adversarial robustness, 100% juridical consistency, and state finality in under 13 seconds. Conversely, System 2 reasoning introduced catastrophic instability, fundamentally driven by a 26.7% Reasoning Non-Convergence (cognitive collapse) rate. This collapse degraded trial-to-trial consensus stability to 72.6% and imposed a 17x latency overhead, introducing critical vulnerabilities to Governance Extractable Value (GEV) and hardware centralization. While rare (1.5% of adversarial trials), we empirically captured "Reasoning-Induced Sycophancy," where the model generated significantly longer internal monologues (averaging 25,750 characters) to rationalize failing the adversarial trap. We conclude that for edge-native SLMs operating under Byzantine Fault Tolerance (BFT) constraints, System 1 parameterized intuition is structurally and economically superior to System 2 iterative deliberation for decentralized consensus.
  Code and Dataset: https://github.com/smarizvi110/sentinel-bench

</details>


### [109] [ClimAgent: LLM as Agents for Autonomous Open-ended Climate Science Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16922)
*Hao Wang,Jindong Han,Wei Fan,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClimAgent是一个基于大型语言模型的自主框架，用于执行广泛的气候研究任务，通过统一的工具使用环境和严格的推理协议，超越了简单的问答系统，能够进行端到端建模和分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化研究面临多尺度数据集快速增长和分析工具复杂化的瓶颈，导致科学发现过程碎片化且劳动密集型。现有大型语言模型应用主要局限于简单的问答任务，忽视了气候科学中复杂的物理约束和数据驱动特性。

Method: 开发了ClimAgent框架，集成了统一的工具使用环境和严格的推理协议。同时提出了ClimaBench基准测试，涵盖2000-2025年间5个不同任务类别的真实世界气候发现挑战问题。

Result: 在ClimaBench上的实验表明，ClimAgent显著优于最先进的基线方法，在解决方案严谨性和实用性方面比原始LLM解决方案提升了40.21%。

Conclusion: ClimAgent为气候科学提供了一个有效的自主研究框架，能够应对真实世界的复杂挑战，填补了现有LLM应用在专业气候科学研究中的空白。

Abstract: Climate research is pivotal for mitigating global environmental crises, yet the accelerating volume of multi-scale datasets and the complexity of analytical tools have created significant bottlenecks, constraining scientific discovery to fragmented and labor-intensive workflows. While the emergence Large Language Models (LLMs) offers a transformative paradigm to scale scientific expertise, existing explorations remain largely confined to simple Question-Answering (Q&A) tasks. These approaches often oversimplify real-world challenges, neglecting the intricate physical constraints and the data-driven nature required in professional climate science.To bridge this gap, we introduce ClimAgent, a general-purpose autonomous framework designed to execute a wide spectrum of research tasks across diverse climate sub-fields. By integrating a unified tool-use environment with rigorous reasoning protocols, ClimAgent transcends simple retrieval to perform end-to-end modeling and analysis.To foster systematic evaluation, we propose ClimaBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for real-world climate discovery. It encompasses challenging problems spanning 5 distinct task categories derived from professional scenarios between 2000 and 2025. Experiments on ClimaBench demonstrate that ClimAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 40.21% improvement over original LLM solutions in solution rigorousness and practicality. Our code are available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/ClimAgent.

</details>


### [110] [Alignment Imprint: Zero-Shot AI-Generated Text Detection via Provable Preference Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16923)
*Junxi Wu,Kailin Huang,Dongjian Hu,Bin Chen,Hao Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Changliang Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于对齐印记的AI生成文本检测方法LAPD，通过标准化信息加权统计量改进现有似然检测方法的稳定性，在实验中相对最强基线提升了45.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于似然的AI生成文本检测方法对内容复杂性敏感且性能不稳定。现代大语言模型经过对齐（包括微调和偏好调整）后会在分布上留下可测量的印记，这为改进检测方法提供了理论依据。

Method: 将对齐过程抽象为一系列约束优化步骤，理论推导对齐印记（Alignment Imprint），即对数似然比可以自然分解为隐式指令偏差和偏好奖励。为解决高熵区域不稳定性问题，提出LAPD（对数似然对齐偏好差异）标准化信息加权统计量，基于对齐印记构建。

Result: 提供了统计保证，证明基于对齐的统计量在性能上优于Fast-DetectGPT。理论证明当对齐模型和基础模型分布接近时，LAPD严格改进未加权的对齐分数。广泛实验表明LAPD相对最强现有基线提升了45.82%，在所有设置中都取得了大而一致的改进。

Conclusion: 通过理论推导对齐印记并开发LAPD标准化加权统计量，提出了一种更稳定、性能更优的AI生成文本检测方法，显著超越了现有基线方法，为解决AI文本检测挑战提供了有效的理论框架和实践方案。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated text is an important but challenging problem. Existing likelihood-based detection methods are often sensitive to content complexity and may exhibit unstable performance. In this paper, our key insight is that modern Large Language Models (LLMs) undergo alignment (including fine-tuning and preference tuning), leaving a measurable distributional imprint. We theoretically derive this imprint by abstracting the alignment process as a sequence of constrained optimization steps, showing that the log-likelihood ratio can naturally decompose into implicit instructional biases and preference rewards. We refer to this quantity as the Alignment Imprint. Furthermore, to mitigate the instability in high-entropy regions, we introduce Log-likelihood Alignment Preference Discrepancy (LAPD), a standardized information-weighted statistic based on alignment imprint. We provide statistical guarantee that alignment-based statistics dominate Fast-DetectGPT in performance. We also theoretically show that LAPD strictly improves the unweighted alignment scores when the aligned and base models are close in distribution. Extensive experiments show that LAPD achieves an improvement 45.82% relative to the strongest existing baselines, yielding large and consistent gains across all settings.

</details>


### [111] [Playing Psychic: Using Thought Trees to Predict Reasoning Models Accuracy on Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16931)
*Jiaxin Fang,Runyuan He,Sahil Bhatia,Neel Gajare,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一个自动生成任意难度编程任务框架，发现推理轨迹的结构（不仅仅是内容）是正确性的强预测指标，并提出了结构化思维树来表示推理轨迹，通过轻量级分类器预测轨迹正确性以提高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型（LLM）通过测试时扩展（使用更多推理令牌）在复杂任务上表现出显著改进，但现有评估主要依赖竞争性编程基准，未能全面反映真实世界的编程能力。研究人员希望系统研究前沿推理模型在真实世界编码基准上的表现，并深入了解其性能特点。

Method: 1）开发了从现有基准自动生成任意难度和结构编码任务的程序化框架；2）提出结构化思维树（structured thought-trees）来表示推理轨迹；3）训练基于思维树特征的轻量级分类器来预测轨迹正确性；4）通过标记和重试结构异常的轨迹来改进性能。

Result: 分析显示推理轨迹的结构（而不仅仅是内容）是正确性的强预测指标。基于思维树特征的分类器能够在较低复杂度级别上通过标记和重试结构异常的轨迹获得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 结构化思维树提供了一种有效表示和分析LLM推理轨迹的方法，强调推理结构在预测模型正确性中的重要性，为改进LLM在编码任务中的性能提供了一种新途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have shown that test-time scaling can substantially improve model performance on complex tasks, particularly in the coding domain.
